{"forms":[{"form":"saeculums","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"saecula","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"According to legend, the gods had allotted a certain number of saecula to every people or civilization; the Etruscans, for example, had been given ten saecula.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A cyclical period of time, roughly equal to the time needed for the complete renewal of a human population:","Any of a sequence of ages (periods of time) such that each age ends with the death of the last person remaining alive since its beginning, and the end of an age marks the beginning of the next."],"qualifier":"originally","tags":["Ancient-Rome","historical"]},{"examples":[{"text":"At the time of the reign of emperor Augustus, the Romans decided that a saeculum was 110 years.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A cyclical period of time, roughly equal to the time needed for the complete renewal of a human population:","Any of a sequence of ages of set length, used to periodise chronicles and track wars."],"tags":["Ancient-Rome","broadly","historical"]},{"glosses":["An approximately 85-year cycle in Strauss-Howe generational theory, a highly controversial sociological theory that postulates that zeitgeist and popular cultural values exist along recurring cycles."]}],"upstream_line":508839,"upstream_offset":2358208733,"upstream_record_sha256":"07a81c69f81b73c772cfe86258af61972318bbd6984bbf0f00e9cd2db6cc4a31","word":"saeculum"}

{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"trapans","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"trepan","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An act of entrapping or tricking; an entrapment; also, a thing which entraps or tricks; a snare or trap; a stratagem or trick."]},{"glosses":["A person (or occasionally an animal) that traps or tricks another into doing something that benefits them but harms the victim; a fraudster, a swindler, a trickster."],"qualifier":"probably originally thieves' cant","synonyms":[{"tags":["US","slang"],"word":"bunco-steerer"},{"word":"charlatan"},{"word":"cheat"},{"word":"jackal"},{"word":"cheater"},{"word":"chiseler"},{"word":"cozener"},{"word":"defrauder"},{"word":"finagler"},{"word":"fleecer"},{"word":"fraud"},{"word":"fraudmeister"},{"word":"fraudster"},{"tags":["slang"],"word":"humbug"},{"tags":["US","dated","slang"],"word":"goldbrick"},{"word":"grifter"},{"tags":["British","obsolete","slang"],"word":"gull"},{"word":"gyp"},{"tags":["obsolete","slang"],"word":"landshark"},{"word":"pickpenny"},{"word":"rook"},{"word":"scammer"},{"word":"scamp"},{"tags":["obsolete"],"word":"Scandaroon"},{"word":"schemester"},{"word":"sharper"},{"word":"slicker"},{"word":"spieler"},{"word":"swindler"},{"tags":["archaic","obsolete"],"word":"trapan"},{"tags":["archaic","obsolete"],"word":"trepan"}],"tags":["archaic","obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/tɹəˈpæn/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]}],"upstream_line":4240223,"upstream_offset":10341756533,"upstream_record_sha256":"904b68ad0e17bcca60677be472b0b5c527a853f8b7aeb5a13669eabb0eabb351","word":"trapan"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"trapans","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"trapanning","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"trapanned","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"trapanned","tags":["past"]},{"form":"trepan","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"glosses":["To catch or entrap (a person or animal) in a snare or trap; to ensnare, to trap."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To trap or trick (someone), especially by using some stratagem, into doing something that benefits the perpetrator but harms the victim; to defraud, to ensnare, to entrap, to swindle."],"tags":["figuratively","transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/tɹəˈpæn/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]}],"upstream_line":4240224,"upstream_offset":10341765589,"upstream_record_sha256":"e5e1ed83253f56df5576d7157c6b59b833619f4c2dcb44dea3bdf81527537b79","word":"trapan"}
